- **plan-restructure**: &gt; Ported universal standard. The mvp planning library had no explicit delta-restructure &gt; skill — when new infrastructure was added (new engine-contracts, QUEUE FABRIC, new &gt; SK-43x), the risk was rewriting correct SESSION phases from scratch and breaking &gt; downstream assumptions. This skill replaces full rewrites with a controlled delta. &gt; TS adaptation for this mvp project: applies to FLOW-XX-RE ([lubochka/xiigen-mvp-engine](https://github.com/lubochka/xiigen-mvp-engine/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/plan-restructure))
- **plan-restructuring**: Etapa final de qualquer planejamento. Normaliza a origem do plano (conversa, docs_dev ou .context/plans) em um diretório próprio dentro de .context/plans/{feature}/, levanta libs internas e serviços externos, coleta documentação atual (context7 + WebSearch/WebFetch) em info_aux_{feature}.md, reestrutura o plano completo, e por fim cria o plano canônico via MCP ai-context (scaffoldPlan + workflow-init) referenciando esses arquivos e deixando o workflow pronto para implementação. Na conclusão, consolida o canônico dentro da pasta e move tudo para archive/.
